commit 02d67f72d81ba201d25a436b9e6b33dd176c177a
Author: stephen swoyer <steve@flox.dev>
Date:   Sat Jul 18 00:58:26 2026 -0400

    Re-audit fixes for 29abba3: parked-session ImportCue bypass, weak pins, doc honesty
    
    The audit of the local fix round found its own gaps:
    
    HIGH — the :import-cue guard checked only the active overlay, but typing
    `:` inside the metadata editor PARKS the session into
    pending_metadata_editor and clears the overlay BEFORE the command
    executes — the typed route (the only one left after the pill removal)
    installed a review that orphaned the parked dirty session. The guard now
    also refuses on pending_metadata_editor.is_some(); refusal leaves the
    overlay None so the command-input Enter handler restores the parked
    editor. Pinned through the parked-session shape.
    
    MEDIUM — two pins could not detect regression: the wheel pin sent
    ScrollDown at the last entry (bounds-refused even unguarded) — now
    ScrollUp with detail_cursor=1, which an unguarded arm moves; the
    GnudbReview pin called the commit helper directly — now drives the REAL
    mouse path through handle_generic_overlay_mouse with computed popup
    coordinates and asserts commit-to-old-row + cursor-moved + edit-cleared.
    
    Honesty — set_pending_source_hint's doc no longer overstates Unstaged
    permissiveness: staging paths degrade to Lost via apply_source_defaults
    immediately, so the delivered guarantee is RETENTION (sentinel survives
    disabled until revalidation), and mid-probe DSD preset staging still
    refuses until the probe completes. Dead is_cue_importable +
    CUE_IMPORTABLE_FIELDS removed (pub fn — invisible to the warning gate).
    
    Gate: suite 4370/0, zero cold-build warnings.
    
    Co-Authored-By: Claude Fable 5 <noreply@anthropic.com>

commit 29abba3a4f860ded0ebd8b36047628fe150ad1ef
Author: stephen swoyer <steve@flox.dev>
Date:   Sat Jul 18 00:31:16 2026 -0400

    Audit fixes for the v6 apply: identity demotion, editor mouse-path corruption, cascade ordering
    
    Six defects from the 4b07d6a three-way audit, fixed per the validated
    plan (design-check corrections folded in):
    
    H1/H2 — source identity must not become Known from guesses or
    emptiness. refresh_source_constraints_preserving_format_selection (the
    verified single choke point) now promotes to Known only from a
    COMPLETED probe (current_info() = Some); Empty sources and pending
    placeholders route through the new set_pending_source_hint (extension
    hint drives row visibility; identity stays Unstaged when fresh, else
    Lost). Previously, emptying the batch or staging an .iso clamped away a
    deliberate same-as-source selection at exactly the moments the
    retention design exists for. Real-path pins added for both.
    
    M5 companion — the LEGITIMATE Known(PCM) clamp is now reported: all
    three probe-completion reducers capture sentinel-selection at entry
    (the clamp can fire at mode-install, not only in the defaults block)
    and set "rate 'source' is invalid for <fmt> with a PCM source; set to
    <rate> Hz" after their own status. Reducer-level pin added.
    
    H3 — DetailEdit wheel-scroll arms now require detail_edit.is_none():
    an unguarded wheel notch moved the commit target under an open inline
    edit, so Enter wrote typed text to a DIFFERENT track (keyboard parity;
    deliberate clicks still commit-first).
    
    H4 — Command::ImportCue refuses while the metadata editor is open (it
    rebuilds a GnudbReview with no editor session and reads the Browse
    selection — running it from the editor destroyed every unsaved edit).
    The :import-cue pill is also removed from both DetailEdit footers and
    the hit-test (renderer/hit-test stay width-aligned).
    
    E — GnudbReview's Enter-commit body extracted into
    commit_gnudb_review_edit_to_row(state, row); row clicks (and the
    double-click reseed) now commit the in-flight edit to the PRE-move row
    before moving the cursor, instead of letting Enter route the buffer to
    whatever was clicked. Noted, unchanged: disc-pill clicks still clear.
    
    M7 — apply_source_info_defaults reorders to cascade -> constraints ->
    auto rules: cascades force-install out-of-range automatic defaults by
    design, so the auto rules previously read unclamped 768k/Int32 and
    armed a real 768->384 resample with resampler=None and a 32->24
    truncation with dither=None. Pin asserts the clamped ALAC case gets
    384k/Int24 WITH a resampler and dither.
    
    Gate: suite 4369/0, zero cold-build warnings, strict depth matrix 4/4.
    
    Co-Authored-By: Claude Fable 5 <noreply@anthropic.com>

commit 4b07d6a176d2b7fa577deac50a9fd59eb5fcbfa2
Author: stephen swoyer <steve@flox.dev>
Date:   Fri Jul 17 23:40:42 2026 -0400

    Apply reasoning-model corrective-v6 bundle: DSF write perf, encrypted-file secrets, MB/queue routing, editor/browse UX (P0+P1+P2)
    
    Applied the cumulative 49-file delivery (manifest 560/560 verified on the
    model's side; clobber triage clean). Lands: bounded in-place DSF tail
    rewrites with journaled recovery, byte progress, mid-copy cancellation
    and a dsf-recover CLI; Linux authenticated encrypted-file secret store
    (ring 0.17) with native macOS/Windows keychains, lazy startup
    resolution, and durable migration; queue-history loading without eager
    secret resolution + transactional re-enqueue of terminal rows;
    :tags-mb plain-file routing; source-aware ReplayGain recomputation;
    tolerant DSF reads with strict writes; shared display-width policy
    (new display_width modules) across browse/overlays/inputs; metadata
    editor Tab/Alt+A/Alt+O inside inline editing; DSD-rate display names;
    GNUDB lifecycle/outside-click hardening; all P2 items incl. symlink-
    correct rollback restore, DST TUI normalization, DFF preflight,
    canonical tag-key shadowing, honest legacy WAV/AIFF carrier, and the
    thread-local sacd-rs allocation counter (former flake now stable).
    
    Apply-side boundary fixes (mechanical): processor.rs rewired to the
    production read_track_metadata_with_warnings; &str/String and integer
    annotations; closure lifetime annotation on the DSF byte-progress
    callback; test-module super:: paths; 15-tuple asserts split (12-element
    trait ceiling); owned reference across a mutable call; renamed settings
    fields; missing test import; dbus already in flake from last round.
    
    Failure adjudication (10 runtime failures classified before fixing):
    - 4 test-authoring bugs: Manual DSD gain was staged via select_value on
      a disabled pill (silent no-op); fixtures now enable the row like
      production and assert the selection took.
    - 2 code gaps the model's tests correctly caught: source-default
      cascades could not install an out-of-range automatic default (the
      clamp-to-allowed contract never engaged) — cascades now force-install
      and the constraint pass clamps with provenance sync; the :set depth
      fixture modeled a probe without applying source defaults.
    - 1 real design collision: the model's own pins require the
      same-as-source rate pill to be ENABLED for DSD targets with no source
      (preset staging), RETAINED-disabled after source loss, and CLAMPED
      AWAY for a known-PCM source — impossible with one bool. Added
      SourceRateIdentity { Unstaged, Known, Lost } threaded through the
      setter, clear, constraint enablement, clamp retention, and the probe
      snapshot; one test updated to use the production setter (outcome
      asserts unchanged).
    - 2 cosmetic pins aligned to actual output (wrapped DSF save-error
      context; lowercase field label in the durability status).
    - Dead-code cleanup: platform-dead native_keyring::set annotated for
      Linux; superseded width helpers pruned.
    
    Gate: workspace suite 4362/0 (sacd-rs allocation flake now stable),
    zero cold-build warnings, TONEPOET_REQUIRE_TOOLS=1 depth matrix 4/4.
    
    Co-Authored-By: Claude Fable 5 <noreply@anthropic.com>

commit 4d3107a2445df103481a1db12074f7d11729e34a
Author: stephen swoyer <steve@flox.dev>
Date:   Fri Jul 17 14:18:25 2026 -0400

    Apply reasoning-model v7 bundle: DSF tagging, source pills, RG policy, GNUDB/editor hardening, secrets (R1-R11, F1-F3, H1-H3)
    
    Applied the corrective-v7 complete-file delivery (35-file surface + new
    src/dsf_tags.rs, src/secret_store.rs; deps id3, keyring, same-file; DB
    v22 metadata-journal migration). Clobber triage confirmed the delivery
    base includes all recent local work (bdb0a43/afffe61 markers intact).
    
    Apply-side fixes at the subset boundary (files the model could not see):
    - plan.rs: restored the Unspecified match arm with its
      unreachable-by-contract rationale (enum lives in source.rs, outside
      the delivery; representation_kind() resolves Unspecified before
      returning).
    - processor.rs: two call sites of the now-fail-closed
      read_single_file_track_metadata map errors to None (advisory
      dispatcher probes; uncertainty already means provisional batch).
    - main.rs: clone non-Copy ReplayGainMode.
    - draw.rs: keychain load failures now render in the settings pane
      ("Keychain unavailable: ...") instead of being silently discarded.
    - flake.nix: dbus for the keyring secret-service backend.
    
    Regressions found by failing pins and FIXED IN CODE (not by weakening
    tests):
    - Same-as-source pills were auto-selected by clamp/fallback: PCM->DSF
      landed the rate on the sentinel instead of a DSD rate, and DSD->PCM
      round trips destroyed the user's staged rate. Automatic clamping now
      skips source-coupled options on both rows (user-opt-in only); new pin
      covers fallback exclusion AND survival of a deliberate source choice.
    - db.rs copy_backup_over truncated the destination before validating
      the rollback marker: a directory/symlink marker emptied the file
      recovery exists to restore (caught by the model's own new test).
      Restore now read-validates first and publishes via temp file + atomic
      rename + parent sync.
    - config.rs recovery validated the target's shape even with zero
      recovery artifacts, converting rename-failure saves into recovery
      errors and making the temp-cleanup path untestable. Target validation
      now runs only when artifacts exist to arbitrate.
    
    Stale pins updated for intended new surface: Dsf visible-rows rate
    counts exclude the sentinel; fingerprint mutation inventory gains
    replay_gain.existing_tags (mutating AWAY from the fixture value); serde
    shape counts re-baselined 79->80 / 86->87 for the one new field.
    
    Gate: workspace suite 4261/0 (one unrelated pre-existing flake:
    sacd-rs dsdiff_dst_adapter allocation-count test is load-sensitive,
    passes 3/3 in isolation, crate untouched by this round), zero
    cold-build warnings, TONEPOET_REQUIRE_TOOLS=1 depth matrix 4/4.
    
    Co-Authored-By: Claude Fable 5 <noreply@anthropic.com>

commit afffe618f4eea53d41689a113c94674d0a4a36dd
Author: stephen swoyer <steve@flox.dev>
Date:   Thu Jul 16 23:07:01 2026 -0400

    Audit fixes for the local round: editor degrade hardening, cue INDEX honesty, archive DSD facts
    
    H1 — the single-cue metadata expansion (keybindings.rs) no longer opens
    the editor on audio OUTSIDE the cue's own folder tree: an alien/stale
    sheet with an absolute FILE ref previously degraded past admission and
    resolved to the original source image elsewhere in the library, so a
    save wrote tags to a file the user never selected. Out-of-tree
    resolutions now fall back to plain expansion (which yields nothing for
    a lone cue and hits the existing "No audio files selected" guard).
    
    H2 — folder rejection sweep (split_cue_album.rs) now examines EVERY cue
    instead of breaking at the first offender: an alien cue that sorted
    first could mask a local malformed cue, and a folder holding a genuine
    admitted cue album plus one alien cue wrongly classified as alien-only
    and degraded to image-level plain editing. SplitCueFolderRejection
    grows folder_admitted_local_members; the alien-only degrade requires
    no rejection referencing in-folder audio AND no admitted local member.
    
    M1 — cue_references_in_folder_audio fails CLOSED on read/parse failure
    (an unreadable cue is the most malformed local case, not an alien one)
    and counts subfolder refs (CD1/image.flac) as in-folder via ancestor
    walk instead of direct-parent equality.
    
    M2 — merged-image cue INDEX times are only emitted when every
    boundary-contributing track (all but the last) has measured samples at
    ONE shared rate; otherwise placeholder zeros instead of silently wrong
    times (DVD-A rates unknown pre-packet-inspection divided by a 44.1k
    guess; a mid-album None shifting every later INDEX early).
    
    M3 — the archive materializer now applies the same DSD container-header
    fact override as the single-file path, so a DSF/DFF delivered inside a
    7z no longer fails post-encode sample-drift validation on ffprobe's
    block-padded duration estimate.
    
    Also: pinned the sinc guard's equality boundary (DSD128->96k,
    DSD256->192k skip the strip; sox rejects cutoff >= rate/2) and
    untangled a doc-comment/#[must_use] mispairing in types.rs.
    
    Tests: all-cues sweep + admitted-member veto, fail-closed unreadable
    cue, degraded merged INDEX (None samples, mixed rates), sinc equality
    pins. Suite 4133/0, zero cold-build warnings.
    
    Co-Authored-By: Claude Fable 5 <noreply@anthropic.com>

commit bdb0a43e151b69351b9941be2df340c684c4b1d7
Author: stephen swoyer <steve@flox.dev>
Date:   Thu Jul 16 22:13:59 2026 -0400

    Local fixes: DSD/DSF conversion chain, published-cue poisoning, tag keys
    
    Fix 1 — DSD->PCM sinc guard (tonepoet-pipeline/src/plugins.rs): skip the
    post-rate noise-strip sinc when the fixed cutoff reaches the target PCM
    Nyquist (sox rejects cutoff >= rate/2; the rate converter's anti-alias
    already bandlimits, same rationale as the DSD512/1024 None policy).
    Unblocks every DSD64->44.1/48k and DSD128/256->low-rate conversion.
    
    DSD probe normalization (types.rs + materializer_single/archive): ffprobe
    reports DSF/DFF BYTE rates (bit rate / 8) and block-padded durations.
    normalize_dsd_probe_rate() scales byte rates x8 with expected_samples in
    lockstep; materializer_single additionally overrides probe facts with the
    exact container-header sample_rate_hz / sample_count_per_channel via
    dsd_source_metadata_from_path. Fixes post-encode "sample rate mismatch"
    and "sample drift" failures on real DSD256 material. Unit pins added.
    
    Fix 2 — companion sweep no longer publishes the synthetic planner
    album.cue (stages.rs companion_source_dir): for synthetic cue-album
    artifacts the source root now resolves through the tracks' real album
    folder, which also repairs the latent bug where synthetic jobs swept no
    real companions at all. build_cue_sheet enriched (CATALOG, PERFORMER,
    TITLE, REM DATE/GENRE, per-track ISRC) and the Merged arm now emits
    correct cumulative INDEX 01 times from expected_samples.
    
    Fix 3 — metadata editor degrades instead of refusing (split_cue_album.rs
    + keybindings.rs): admission rejections carry a structured
    references_in_folder_audio signal; alien cues (no in-folder audio refs)
    fold into a status warning and the plain editor opens; local malformed
    cues keep the atomic refusal.
    
    Fix 4 — tag keys converge on foobar/flac convention: pipeline writes
    TRACKTOTAL/DISCTOTAL (legacy spellings dual-read everywhere, both in
    AUTHORITATIVE_CUE_MANAGED_TAG_KEYS so re-runs converge legacy outputs);
    editor canon flipped (TOTALTRACKS->TRACKTOTAL, TOTALDISCS->DISCTOTAL,
    DESCRIPTION->COMMENT); apply_comment_changes removes ALL alias spellings
    of an edited key so edits no longer leave stale duplicates.
    
    Fix 5 — removed the "FLAC 1.4+/ffmpeg 6.1+" hint suffix from the
    bit-depth row (draw_output.rs).
    
    Verified: workspace suite green (~4130 tests, 0 failed), zero cold-build
    warnings, TONEPOET_REQUIRE_TOOLS=1 depth matrix green. Real-tree probes:
    DSD256 L.A. Woman track -> FLAC 352.8k/24-bit full duration; DSOTM
    wv->FLAC publishes no synthetic cue, sweeps real companions,
    TRACKTOTAL=10 with no legacy spellings.
    
    Co-Authored-By: Claude Fable 5 <noreply@anthropic.com>

commit 7eb466e1368607d99faa485840a46d7e396897c0
Author: stephen swoyer <steve@flox.dev>
Date:   Thu Jul 16 19:15:39 2026 -0400

    Lossy CUE images: measure the tail decode instead of trusting the header
    
    CUE segment staging validated exact sample counts derived from the
    image's container header (ffprobe duration_ts), but a lossy decode
    differs in both directions: the decoder trims encoder delay/padding
    the header includes (MP3+CUE failed at Materialize with "has N
    samples, expected M"), and a Xing-less VBR header can UNDERSTATE, in
    which case the old capped extraction silently truncated real audio and
    validated clean.
    
    Fix, scoped to the one segment whose length is genuinely unknowable —
    the last track of a lossy image (CUE tail semantics: play to end of
    audio):
    
    - SegmentLengthPolicy { Exact, LossyTail } on stage_cue_segment_as_wav;
      LossyTail stages OPEN-ENDED (atrim=start_sample only) so the decoded
      length is captured whether the header over- or understates
    - the length is measured once on the tmp file (new
      measure_staged_cue_segment_as: every integrity check except the
      count); a shortfall beyond max(sample_rate/10, 8192) samples fails
      closed as a truncated source; overage beyond 1s logs a warning and
      keeps the audio
    - every later validation runs exact against the MEASURED count, and
      the measured value is backfilled into all facts: the staged filename
      (n{measured}), CueSegmentCarrier.samples, and expected_samples — so
      D5 post-encode sample validation, progress, and logs stay exact by
      construction with zero changes
    - interior segments (INDEX-bounded) and all lossless images keep the
      strict exact behavior unchanged; boundaries now carry is_image_tail
      (one tail per image file, multi-image unified layouts correct)
    - accepted trade-off (commented): the pre-ffmpeg reuse short-circuit
      cannot hit for lossy tails on interrupted-run retry (the published
      file lives under the measured name) — one extra decode, correctness
      unaffected
    
    Tests: the KNOWN-GAP regression is un-ignored and extended to two
    tracks (interior exact + measured tail, both publish at the 24-bit
    lossy default); five new unit tests pin shortfall-within-bound
    backfill, beyond-bound truncation failure, overage keep, exact-policy
    strictness, and the measured-name rewrite helper.
    
    Verified: workspace suite 4118/0, zero cold warnings, strict matrix
    green (TONEPOET_REQUIRE_TOOLS=1, incl. the un-ignored lossy test);
    live CLI probes: 2-track MP3+CUE -> FLAC publishes 73500 (exact
    interior) + 58800 (measured tail, 1341 short of header) samples at
    24-bit; AAC+CUE -> FLAC publishes the decode-true length.
    
    Co-Authored-By: Claude Fable 5 <noreply@anthropic.com>

commit f8009b672a3051d71d49e17b5be7d4763bc23f96
Author: stephen swoyer <steve@flox.dev>
Date:   Wed Jul 15 22:07:54 2026 -0400

    Fourth audit round: WavPack Int24 routing, dark-test resurrection, planner strip fix
    
    Audit of the third round resolved the WavPack contradiction empirically
    and uncovered two systemic problems: a fifth silent-substitution class
    member on the DEFAULT path, and months of dark sub-crate tests.
    
    WavPack truth, settled with real encoders (all cross-checked against
    wvunpack -s):
    - ffprobe bits_per_raw_sample IS container-accurate for .wv (native
      24-bit probes 24) — round 2's "decoded s32p" premise was wrong as
      stated; only the sample_fmt fallback is misleading
    - ffmpeg's wavpack encoder cannot write 24-bit AT ALL: Int24 requests
      store true 32-bit ints (16 and 32 are faithful) — a REAL substitution
      (broadening) sitting on PreferredTool::Auto's no-processing path
      (7z FLAC albums, Blu-ray, post-SSRC): with wvunpack present every
      track encodes then fails D5; without it the 32-bit file publishes
      silently
    
    Fixes:
    - FfmpegPlugin::supports returns UNSUPPORTED for WavPack+Int24 (hybrid
      exempt — that path delegates to the native wavpack CLI); routing
      falls to sox, which writes true 24-bit; pin tests cover both tools,
      both processing modes, Int16/Int32 eligibility, and the hybrid
      exemption
    - wavpack_log_only_measured_depth: float .wv probes as fltp with
      bits_per_raw_sample=32 (a byte width) — classify float before
      trusting raw bits; recovered integer measurement it wrongly discarded
    - conversion_summary target side INTERPRETS the 33/320/640 float
      conventions (matching the source side) instead of dropping them;
      Source-resolved float targets now label "32-bit float"
    - DSD round-trip rate restore: FormatState remembers the PCM rate on
      entering DSD and restores it on exit (deliberate downsamples
      preserved), source-rate fallback otherwise; :set format now routes
      through after_user_selection + the DSD-source cascade instead of a
      half-mirror (keyboard/:set parity); regression tests for both
    - prune_redundant_metadata_steps: a strip-mode MetadataTransfer (both
      policy flags false) was vacuously "satisfied" and pruned — the plan
      renamed the source with tags intact. Never prune strip steps.
    - Dropped the unreachable warn in finish_browse_queue_review
    
    Dark-test resurrection (plain `cargo test` only tests the root package;
    sub-crate tests had been dark — planning.rs since the
    container_ffmpeg_flags commit, backend/features since extraction):
    - tonepoet-pipeline/tests/planning.rs: field fix + 5 stale pins updated
      to intentional evolutions (typed metadata-effect pruner, FLAC default
      compression 5->8, DSD -u flag) after forensic triage of each
    - backend tests/examples: hexload-era crate names renamed, struct/
      signature drift patched, 3 inverted resample-quality pins corrected
      to the production 0=Ultra..4=LQ scale, target_sample_rate contract
    - features tests/examples: ConversionResult/log-writer drift, wording
      pin ("concurrent")
    - wizard theme-contract tests repointed to step 0 (screen that hosts
      the disabled format-options rows)
    - settings_fingerprint serde sentinel re-baselined (56->79 / 63->86)
    - MetadataPlanEffect re-exported; serde-gated default_true cfg'd
    - CLAUDE.md: document `cargo test --workspace` as the suite command
    
    Workspace suite 4038/0 (633 previously-dark tests now running), zero
    cold-build warnings.

commit 1bdf9d1a3b653aef5482a1d8fc149f7610ad4b20
Author: stephen swoyer <steve@flox.dev>
Date:   Wed Jul 15 20:37:38 2026 -0400

    Third audit round: rate-fallback downsample, WavPack measurement, log durability
    
    Adversarial audit of 5ded1f2 found the round-2 rate clamp traded one
    wrong rate for a worse one, plus refinements verified empirically:
    
    - DSD->PCM format fallback now restores the probed PCM source rate:
      a 96 kHz FLAC source round-tripping the format pill through DSF
      landed on 44.1 (silent downsample armed via auto-resampler) because
      the clamp has no source context and the DSD->PCM cascade only covers
      DSD sources. after_user_selection and :set format capture the
      pre-change rate and re-select the source rate when a DSD rate fell
      back to PCM; DSD sources refuse (their rate is not a PCM option) and
      keep the existing cascade. Regression test covers the round trip.
    - clamp_sample_rate_pill uses DsdRate::from_hz membership instead of a
      duplicated 2_822_400 literal
    - WavPack no-expectation measurement recovered: bits_per_raw_sample is
      container-accurate for .wv (verified against wvunpack -s on native
      and ffmpeg encodes); the blanket None discarded correct data and hid
      real depth broadening (ffmpeg's wavpack encoder writes 32-bit from
      s32 input). New wavpack_log_only_measured_depth trusts only that
      field; unit tests pin both directions
    - conversion_summary target-depth fallback filters the legacy 320/640
      float convention like the source side already did (no "320-bit"
      rendering one probe outage away)
    - Expansion warnings are logged on finish_browse_queue_review's two
      early returns (previously dropped with no status and no log), the
      preset-failure return cleans synthetic CUE artifacts (leak), and the
      currently-unreachable overwrite branch in
      handle_browse_convert_expansion_complete logs before its status is
      clobbered
    
    Suite 3405/0, zero cold warnings.

commit 9ef8dad9277acdd6aafa980326a6e6804a3e8496
Author: stephen swoyer <steve@flox.dev>
Date:   Wed Jul 15 19:26:37 2026 -0400

    Audit fixes for depth-honesty v9 apply: close residual substitution doors
    
    Four-way adversarial audit of the v9 apply (80d0d25) found the silent
    bit-depth substitution class still escaping through three side doors,
    plus lifecycle/UX defects. All verified mechanically before fixing.
    
    Depth honesty:
    - plan.rs: reject_unsupported_resolved_depth() after both
      resolve_target_bit_depth sites — BitDepthTarget::Source resolving to
      ALAC+Int32 / WavPack+Float now fails closed at plan time instead of
      silently downgrading at the encoder (validate() only sees Pcm targets)
    - plan.rs: DSD direct sox-encode branch excludes the silently-
      substituting combos (FLAC+Int32, AIFF+float, WavPack+float)
    - plan_bridge.rs: split the two consumers of "source depth" — encode
      planning keeps carrier-first (the realized s32 segment needs explicit
      depth args; dropping it made FLAC Int16 emit 24-bit), while
      BitDepthTarget::Source resolution reads only the true probed track
      depth, falling back to the format default, never the carrier width;
      DSD targets keep Source unresolved
    - stages.rs: PostEncodeDepthExpectation { depth, class_strict } —
      explicit Pcm targets stay class-strict; Source targets compare bits
      only (float sources probe as plain 32; SourceAudioCoding has no Float
      variant), ending false D5 failures
    - stages.rs: wvunpack oracle absence (spawn failure) degrades to a
      logged skip instead of failing the track; parser bails per-line, and
      the validator soft-skips WavPack when no measurement is available
    - stages.rs: resolved_target_bit_depth reverted to track.bit_depth only
      (naming-template fence); conversion-log fallback is log-local
    
    Lifecycle:
    - mod.rs: both commit_batch rollback sites subtract skip-transferred
      artifacts (registered to live items) from caller ownership — they
      were returned to the caller AND owned by the manager (double delete)
    
    TUI honesty:
    - command.rs: expansion warnings fold into the queued status instead of
      being overwritten; :Commit start path retains detect errors;
      :set depth reports refusal for format-disabled depths
    - pill.rs: select_value() returns whether an enabled option was chosen
    - app.rs: clamp_pill scans downward first (FLAC 32 -> ALAC lands on 24)
    
    New tests: settings validation arms (ALAC32/WV-float rejected, FLAC32/
    AIFF-float honored), plan-time resolved-depth rejection. Suite
    3400/0 green; cold build zero warnings.

    Audit fixes for the G+H apply round (7 findings across 4 reviewers)
    
    Four-way adversarial audit of d6ff18c; every finding below was
    mechanically verified before fixing:
    
    - HIGH: canonical_metadata_display_key's fallback squashed separators
      out of every unknown key — `:a REPLAYGAIN_ALBUM_GAIN` (any editor)
      created and WROTE a separator-less REPLAYGAINALBUMGAIN tag, and
      unified surfaces displayed mangled names for all non-alias tags.
      The fallback now preserves the key (uppercased); alias lookup keeps
      the squashed comparison.

      subset while the MB populate applies positionally from row 0 — an
      unguarded Browse lookup for one member image racing an open unified
      editor (or a guarded non-first-group apply) would overwrite the
      FIRST group's rows with the other group's titles and persist the
      corruption. Subset acceptance now requires the contiguous row
      PREFIX; non-prefix groups get the rerun status (group-offset-aware
      apply is noted as follow-up).
    - G1 authority admitted embedded sheets with duplicate/discontinuous
      TRACK numbers or non-increasing INDEX 01 that the materializer later
      rejects — turning a plan-time sidecar fallback into a convert-time
      group failure. Authority now runs the same structural validation.
    - Alias normalization + replaced-key removal made loaded Year/Album
      Artist/Genre values vanish from display AND revert when the cue
      model lacked them; the builder now harvests loaded album-key values
      (post-normalization) and seeds the upserts, sheet precedence intact.
    - The H2 ISRC pollution signature was computed against the
      PRE-projection (sidecar) sheet — dead on exactly the trees F2
      polluted. Authority projection now runs before the cleanup plan is
      derived, so signatures compare against the embedded sheet's ISRCs.
    - Detail-overlay paste replicated a single line across ALL slots for
      every file-dim field (pasting one title into a 10-file editor
      overwrote all ten) and allowed distinct per-file ALBUM values;
      replication is again restricted to album-scoped keys.
    - H15 sweep completed: bulk-rename Apply-CUE and AccurateRip cue
      discovery now use the shared classifier predicate (AppleDouble
      `._album.cue` no longer wins or aborts those flows), the picks are
      deterministic, and tui's is_user_visible_cue_path delegates to the
      classifier so the policy has one owner. Stale grow-or-shrink comment
      in MB populate corrected.

    album title. Suite: 3392 passed / 0 failed; zero cold-build warnings.

    Five confirmed findings from the four-way apply audit of d28f081, fixed
    with regression tests:
    
    - ConvertCustom on an unprobed Archive .iso was hijacked by the disc
      dispatch (extension acceptance introduced for explicit disc actions
      leaked into a shared action) — now strict-disc-only there, so generic
      archive-ISO conversion falls through to its handler again.

      update_item_status (drop + TTL scavenger remain the backstops).

      status message raced the processor's queue write lock (try_write
      miss). The same deferred branch now recovers it on the next
      uncontended terminal delivery.
    - Context-menu "Retry failed" flipped statuses in place across the
      completed history, stranding permanently-Queued rows the processor
      never scans and persistence resurrected. New
      ConversionQueue::retry_all_failed moves records back into the active
      queue; the menu action now routes through it.
    - :view error path never forced a redraw after the terminal-restore
      guard cleared the screen (blank TUI on viewer spawn failure).

commit 36ff51ad969abbe839e010e47496ffacb4702665
Author: stephen swoyer <steve@flox.dev>
Date:   Tue Jul 14 19:43:03 2026 -0400

    Clear unified-surface dirty state after all member images save
    
    User-reported during real-tree verification: after Alt+A saved the
    unified DSOTM album, Alt+O would not close the editor and Esc raised
    "Discard unsaved metadata changes?".
    
    Root cause: reduce_saved_slots only advanced per_file_originals for
    file-aligned entries (values dimensioned by path count) and for the
    single-file case. Unified CUE-album per-track rows are dimensioned by
    TRACK count (10 rows vs 2 member images), so a fully successful save
    never advanced their originals; presentation_tab_has_changes stayed
    true and the surface was permanently dirty — the editor could never
    close cleanly.
    
    Row-dimensioned entries persist through the regenerated embedded
    CUESHEET written to every member image, so once every image slot saved
    they are durable: mark them saved (same justification the deleted-row
    arm already used). Partial saves still keep the surface dirty for
    retry.
    
    Two regression tests: full save clears dirty; partial save keeps rows
    pending.
    
    Co-Authored-By: Claude Fable 5 <noreply@anthropic.com>

commit f1a078083677d4afdfbe6167a7337460bef67c8f
Author: stephen swoyer <steve@flox.dev>
Date:   Tue Jul 14 19:17:06 2026 -0400

    Case-insensitive common-title prefix for split-cue album merges
    
    Real-tree acceptance on the DSOTM rip caught this: the two sides title
    their cues "The Dark Side of the Moon (Japan Toshiba Harvest-Odeon
    EOP-80778 LP / 24-192)" and "The Dark Side Of The Moon (Side B)". The
    case-sensitive prefix compare cut at "of"/"Of", and the trailing
    split-designator strip then ate "Side", so the merged album title came
    out as "The Dark" (wrong editor ALBUM value, wrong synthetic sheet
    TITLE, wrong output folder name).
    
    The prefix walk now compares characters case-insensitively while
    preserving the first title's casing, yielding "The Dark Side of the
    Moon" on the real tree. Regression test added with the exact real-tree
    title pair.
    
    Suite: 3296 passed / 0 failed; zero cold-build warnings.
    
    Co-Authored-By: Claude Fable 5 <noreply@anthropic.com>

commit a7251a2c962acaec3b59b6151537e85711418302
Author: stephen swoyer <steve@flox.dev>
Date:   Tue Jul 14 19:04:58 2026 -0400

    Unified synthetic cue album: one surface, one sheet, one output album
    
    Implements the round-4 model from docs/unified_synthetic_cue_album_brief.md
    (v18 bundle apply): same-folder split-cue albums now present and convert as
    ONE album, not per-side surfaces.
    
    Core model:
    - Metadata editor opens ONE flattened surface (no tabs/selector) for merged
      cue groups: MetadataEditorModel::single_surface + CueAlbumSyntheticSheet
      with per-row CueAlbumTrackSource mapping (cue path, image, local index,
      original track number, INDEX 00/01, ISRC).
    - Save writes ONE concatenated multi-FILE cue sheet (TRACK 01..N continuous,
      INDEX times local to each image, parse-normal PERFORMER emission for
      byte-stable parse-generate round-trips, 99-track cue ceiling fails closed)
      identically to EVERY member image's embedded CUESHEET.
    - Folder conversion publishes ONE album folder (merged title, continuous
      track numbers) via synthetic album.cue artifacts under
      tonepoet-synthetic-cue-albums with owner-lock lifecycle and live-owner-safe
      scavenging; ownership transfers through QueueExpansionResult to the
      ConversionManager by item ID.
    - Explicit right-click Convert on a single .cue bypasses all grouping
      heuristics and keeps side identity.
    - New shared policy module src/convert/split_cue_album.rs hosts the grouping
      ladder (title prefix -> concat-TOC hit merges -> per-cue distinct hits
      split -> conservative merge; probe miss never splits) with
      order-independent decision keys.
    - MB population for unified surfaces dimensions per-track entries by row and
      album entries by file, with bounds-guarded slot writes.
    - Cuesheet regenerate/delete/edit wired through menu + help discoverability.
    
    Applier fixes on top of the bundle:
    - Real production gap: queue-expansion planner now refuses to merge two cues
      referencing the same image (alternate track layouts of one rip stay on the
      legacy per-cue path).
    - Parse-normal PERFORMER inheritance in the generator (parser inherits album
      performer, generator must emit it for byte stability).
    - Rewrote tests/unified_synthetic_cue_output_boundary.rs to real sine FLAC
      fixtures + RealToolRunner with tool-availability skip guards: planned
      ffmpeg/sox commands run the real streaming binaries (progress parsing
      bypasses the ToolRunner seam), so the bundle's stub-runner harness could
      never intercept the encode step.
    - Fixture/assertion corrections (2-track cue registry fixtures, SingleSurface
      tab contract, ISRC upsert fixtures, source-scan span end marker, canonical
      synthetic artifact layout in terminal test) and dead-code cleanup with
      #[cfg(test)] retention for test-only wrappers.
    
    Suite: 3295 passed / 0 failed; zero cold-build warnings.
    
    Co-Authored-By: Claude Fable 5 <noreply@anthropic.com>

    Apply embedded-cuesheet bundle + fix six test/wiring defects it could not see
    
    Reasoning-model bundle embedded_cuesheet_delete_confirm_fixed:
    
    - Bug 1: MB-apply flow detects multi-cue albums and opens/populates the
      tabbed surface editor (build_metadata_editor_for_cue_surfaces_with_mb_release,
      populate_split_cue_metadata_editor_from_mb_release slicing the release
      tracklist across tabs); stale embedded CUESHEETs describing one side no
      longer shape the album.
    - Bug 2: in-editor :tags-mb gains split-cue awareness with the album text
      fallback (no more bare single-side TOC misses).
    - Feature A: :cuesheet-delete stages an embedded-CUESHEET tag tombstone
      (with confirm; sidecar file preserved) and reshapes the editor.
    - Feature B: :cuesheet-edit extracts the embedded CUESHEET to a temp file,
      opens the SYSTEM editor via the existing open_in_editor machinery,
      parse-validates on return, and stages the write-back.
    - Feature C: the album-grouping heuristic ladder (title shared-prefix ->
      concat-TOC probe hit = merge -> per-cue distinct hits = split ->
      conservative merge; probe misses never split), cached by TOC string,
      wired into metadata surface grouping, GNUDB dispatch, and MB dispatch.
    
    Apply fixes (the sandbox cannot compile or run tests):
    - open_editor_with_mb_release gained tx-dependent apply plumbing but not
      the tx parameter; threaded through its six callers (+ a moved-value
      clone in the GNUDB ladder fallthrough).
    - fixture_cue wrote 'INDEX 01 00:60:00'-style stamps (seconds >= 60) that
      its own validator rightly rejects; proper mm:ss split (fixed four tests).
    - The distinct-hits ladder test used two parts with IDENTICAL durations,
      so their per-cue TOC strings collided in the cache and the ladder
      correctly refused to split; durations now vary per part as real sides do.
    - REAL regression its dispatch change introduced: split-cue MB population
      ran for ANY multi-tab editor, silently replacing the apply-to-matching-
      presentations flow for disc editors (SACD areas / DVD-A groups repeat
      the same tracks per tab — slicing across tabs is wrong there). New
      MetadataEditorState::cue_surface_tabs marker set by the cue-surface
      builder gates the population; the pre-existing apply-all test pinned it.
    - Test-only grouping composition gated #[cfg(test)] (production call
      sites inline the sequence).
    
    Suite: 3253/0 (24 net new tests). Zero cold-build warnings.

    Apply browse source-coverage bundle (v7) + close four gaps it could not test
    
    Reasoning-model bundle browse_source_coverage_worldclass_fixed_v7 (its

    - classify_file extended with ape/dsf/dff/shn/ogg/oga/tta; AudioFormat
      gains decode-only Shorten/Ogg/Tta with a source-vs-output model:
      input_decodable() covers sources, all()/output_encodable()/common/

    - All-CUE discovery replaces first-cue-wins (gnudb::find_cues_in_dir,
      accuraterip::find_cue_files, cue_parser::detect_single_image_cues)
      with per-cue FILE-reference pairing (never sort order); ambiguous
      same-stem fallbacks fail closed, literal references stay authoritative.
    - Multi-cue folders: metadata editor opens presentation tabs per cue
      surface; Browse GNUDB runs per-cue lookups; Browse MB uses album text
      search instead of synthesizing a whole-album TOC from side images.
    - ISO coherence: unprobed .iso entries get bounded synchronous disc
      classification at context-menu/action time (no archive semantics for
      disc images); SACD ISO gains the Convert (default stream) action;
      EntryKind owns the disc-source predicate.
    - Source admission centralized in src/convert/source_admission.rs; the
      manual file-input walk, queue expansion, materializer image admission,
      and FormatDetector all delegate to the shared policy; semantic subset
      lists (lossy/preemphasis/HDCD heuristics) documented as local.

      out-of-slice files (plan_bridge, unified_request, wizard,
      wizard_integration, convert_actions, presets) — each extended following
      the pre-existing Ape decode-only arm conventions.
    - Its split-cue OGG/TTA test used placeholder bytes, but SingleImageInfo
      detection genuinely probes the image (ffprobe); fixtures now encode
      real 2s sine images via ffmpeg with cue indexes inside the audio.
    - LATENT pre-existing defect newly reachable: probe_audio_image's
      -count_frames ffprobe decodes the ENTIRE image, and its 30s timeout
      (fine for FLAC) cannot decode a Monkey's Audio CD image; raised to
      600s with the reasoning documented (header-read probes stay at 30s).
    
    Live acceptance (real trees):
    - CCR Chronicle (two subfolders, APE image + cue each): 2/2 cues
      decomposed, 40 tracks, per-cue album folders.
    - DSOTM (two cue/wv side pairs in one folder): 2/2 sides decomposed with

      test pending.

    or handle split-cue folders (classify_tags_mb_paths passes the dir into
    PathKind::Audio); TUI is the split-cue tagging surface for now.

+# Implementation report: DSF pills hardening round — corrective audit v7
+
+## Delivery status and source limitation
+
+This archive supersedes corrective v6. It addresses the stale-configuration recovery blocker: ambiguous backup selection, nondurable restore ordering, link-following candidate inspection, and silently discarded enumeration or cleanup failures.
+
+The available source artifact remains the 35-file delivery subset rather than the complete HEAD repository described by the brief. It contains no `.git`, `docs/`, or `CLAUDE.md`. I therefore could not perform the required commit-level adversarial review of `7eb466e`, `6a56090`, `bdb0a43`, and `afffe61`, nor inspect omitted call sites. That requirement remains outstanding for application to the actual full tree.
+
+No Rust compiler or Cargo runner was available. I did not run `cargo fmt`, `cargo test --workspace`, a cold warning-free build, or `TONEPOET_REQUIRE_TOOLS=1 cargo test --test depth_format_matrix`; I do not claim those acceptance commands pass.
+
+## Corrective v7 outcomes
+
+### 1. Recovery no longer guesses among replacement backups
+
+`recover_stale_config_artifacts()` no longer sorts replacement backups by optional filesystem modification time.
+
+While holding the existing store lock, it now:
+
+- propagates `read_dir()` failure and every individual directory-entry error;
+- identifies matching temporary and replacement-backup artifacts deterministically;
+- rejects symbolic links and all other non-regular artifact types using no-follow metadata;
+- opens backup candidates without following the final link and validates pathname-to-handle identity;
+- reads every backup candidate and requires it to be valid UTF-8 and deserialize as `TonepoetConfig`;
+- sorts candidates only to produce deterministic diagnostics, never to choose authority; and
+- when the target is absent, restores only if exactly one valid backup exists.
+
+Two or more valid candidates are now an explicit ambiguous state. Recovery returns an error naming every candidate and leaves the target, backups, temporary files, secret journal, and credential store untouched.
+
+A malformed or symlinked replacement backup likewise stops recovery without cleanup or secret reconciliation.
+
+### 2. A restored configuration becomes durable before it controls credentials
+
+A single valid backup is no longer moved directly into place and immediately trusted.
+
+Recovery now:
+
+1. creates a unique owner-only recovery temporary file;
+2. writes the validated backup bytes;
+3. calls `sync_all()` on that file;
+4. renames it to the missing target;
+5. calls the publication-specific parent-directory durability barrier; and only then
+6. considers removal of the original backup or any stale temporary file.
+
+If the post-rename directory barrier fails, recovery returns an exact error stating that durability is unconfirmed. The original replacement backup is retained. Because `load_from_path()` invokes recovery before `reconcile_config_secret_publication_locked()`, the pending secret-publication journal and every referenced credential remain untouched.
+
+Before deleting stale artifacts, recovery also requires the parent publication barrier to be available. This makes unsupported platforms fail before deletion rather than removing the only recovery authority and then reporting that durability could not be established. After deletion, the parent directory is synchronized again. Secret reconciliation begins only after both the target publication and all recovery cleanup have completed durably.
+
+### 3. Cleanup and inspection failures are authoritative
+
+Recovery no longer uses `entries.flatten()`, `let _ = remove_file(...)`, `Path::exists()`, or optional timestamp metadata in its decision path.
+
+It now propagates:
+
+- directory enumeration failures;
+- individual entry failures;
+- no-follow metadata failures;
+- candidate open, identity, read, UTF-8, and TOML-validation failures;
+- recovery temporary creation, write, file-sync, rename, and cleanup failures;
+- stale temporary or obsolete backup removal failures; and
+- both pre-cleanup and post-cleanup directory-durability failures.
+
+A failed recovery-temporary cleanup is combined with the primary publication error rather than replacing or hiding it.
+
+### 4. Existing published targets remain authoritative only when valid
+
+When a regular target already exists and replacement backups are present, recovery validates the published target before deleting any backup. A malformed or non-regular target therefore cannot cause valid recovery authority to be silently discarded. Once the target parses, it is the authoritative published state and stale backups may be removed under the durable cleanup protocol.
+
+## Tests added or strengthened in corrective v7
+
+- `two_valid_replacement_backups_fail_closed_without_selecting_by_timestamp` — creates two different valid configurations and asserts the exact ambiguity error, absent target, and retention of both candidates.
+- `malformed_replacement_backup_is_rejected_without_cleanup` — asserts the exact validation error and exact retained malformed bytes.
+- `symlinked_replacement_backup_is_rejected_without_following_target` — Unix boundary pin proving the link and its target remain unchanged.
+- `recovery_sync_failure_retains_backup_journal_and_credential_authority` — injects failure at the directory barrier after recovery rename and asserts the exact error, retained original backup, retained pending journal, exact surviving secret, and credential count.
+- `stale_temporary_cleanup_failure_is_visible_and_retains_secret_authority` — injects a removal failure and asserts the exact error, exact stale bytes, retained journal, exact secret, and credential count.
+- `single_valid_backup_is_restored_durably_before_cleanup` — asserts exact restored configuration bytes and removal of the backup only after successful durable recovery.
+- `config_save_removes_stale_temporary_files` — now uses a syntactically and structurally valid replacement backup, so the test exercises the actual validation and recovery policy rather than relying on arbitrary bytes.
+
+The stale-artifact cleanup tests that require a successful parent-directory durability barrier are Unix-only. Ambiguity and malformed-candidate validation remain cross-platform. No behavior assertion was weakened or removed.
+
+## Files changed in corrective v7
+
+- `src/config.rs` — replace timestamp-based stale recovery with fail-closed candidate validation; add no-follow regular-file and identity checks; implement durable copy-and-publish recovery; make every enumeration, validation, cleanup, and directory-sync failure visible; add fault injection and value-level recovery tests.
+- `IMPLEMENTATION_REPORT_DSF_PILLS_HARDENING.md` — this corrective v7 report.
+
+All other files are retained complete and unchanged from corrective v6 so the archive remains directly applicable as the same complete-file delivery surface.
+
+## Earlier corrective work retained
+
+The archive retains the v1-v6 corrections, including:
+
+- actual DSF container I/O through generic `id3` stream APIs;
+- DSF structural, pointer, ID3-extent, and trailing-data validation;
+- atomic DSF publication and refusal to mutate through symbolic links;
+- persistent native keyring feature selection and production mock-backend rejection;
+- whole-transaction config and MRU store locking;
+- crash-recoverable pending secret publication;
+- deterministic config credential slots and queue migration references;
+- retryable transient keychain failures;
+- explicit set, retain, and clear config-secret semantics;
+- safe `fs2` locking with immutable validated lock markers and no handwritten FFI;
+- strict public configuration save behavior on unconfirmed durability;
+- conservative Windows publication classification;
+- complete bounded configuration-symlink-chain resolution;
+- metadata-file durability before rollback-journal commit;
+- production-shaped R8 coverage;
+- behavioral ReplayGain runner-count tests;
+- fail-closed DSF materializer metadata errors;
+- no-clobber metadata rollback authority and state-aware recovery;
+- source-relative format-pill corrections; and
+- the supplied implementations for R1-R11, F1-F3, and H1-H3 within the 35-file surface.
+
+Integration with files absent from the supplied artifact remains unreviewed.
+
+## P1 recommendation
+
+Recommendation remains **(b): suppress source `.cue` companions for Tracks-layout output**.
+
+Keeping source cues preserves provenance but publishes `FILE` references to images absent from the output. Suppression is the smallest reversible change and leaves the generated per-track cue authoritative. Rewriting could preserve richer provenance but needs a separately designed reconciliation path.
+
+P1 was not implemented. The brief leaves the decision open and permits implementation only for option (b); this delivery records the recommendation without silently changing product behavior.
+
+## Assumptions and deliberate limits
+
+- Exactly one valid replacement backup is authoritative only when the published target is absent. Multiple plausible backups are never ordered by timestamps or guessed from contents.
+- A present, regular, parseable target is the published authority. Backups may be cleaned only after that target has been validated.
+- Recovery backups are copied into an owner-only temporary file rather than renamed directly, so the original authority remains available until publication durability is established.
+- Stale temporary files are not candidate configuration authority; they must be regular files and are removed only under the durable cleanup protocol. Replacement backups and any published target used to resolve them must parse as `TonepoetConfig`.
+- Platforms without a supported parent-directory publication barrier cannot complete stale-artifact deletion or backup recovery. They fail visibly and retain recovery authority rather than claiming completion.
+- General cross-store secret garbage collection remains outside this round. Recovery only controls credentials through the already journaled store transaction.
+- No DSF artwork mutation, DFF tagging, or P1 product behavior change was added.
+- The missing complete repository and Git history were not reconstructed or guessed.
+
+## Static verification performed
+
+Without a Rust toolchain, verification was by inspection and structural checks:
+
+- inspected every call to `recover_stale_config_artifacts()` and confirmed recovery precedes secret reconciliation;
+- confirmed timestamp ordering, flattened directory entries, and ignored recovery removals are absent;
+- confirmed matching backup candidates are opened with final-component no-follow behavior and checked as regular files;
+- traced the exact states for zero, one, and multiple backups; valid, malformed, and symlinked candidates; target-present cleanup; post-rename sync failure; stale-removal failure; and retry after an unconfirmed restore;
+- confirmed the original backup remains after a post-rename sync failure;
+- confirmed cleanup is preceded and followed by the publication-specific parent-directory barrier;
+- confirmed all newly added `expect` calls are test-only and no new production `unsafe`, FFI, `panic!`, `unwrap()`, or `expect()` was introduced;
+- parsed `Cargo.toml` with Python's TOML parser;
+- checked the changed Rust file for balanced lexical delimiters and all text files for trailing whitespace; and
+- extracted the final archive and compared every included file byte-for-byte against the delivery tree.
+
+These checks do not substitute for Rust compilation or the acceptance suite.
+
+## Complete files included in the archive
+
+The archive retains the complete 35-file delivery surface from corrective v6, including this report and all previously delivered complete source files. No partial patches, elisions, or “rest unchanged” markers are included.

-**CLI depth policy:** `--bit-depth` accepts `16`, `24`, `32`, `32f`, `64f`, or `source`. With no flag, the CLI retains `source`. For DSD and lossy inputs, where a PCM source width is undefined, `source` resolves to the target format's conservative PCM default (24-bit for FLAC, ALAC, WavPack, WAV, and AIFF) and the conversion log identifies that value as a plan default. +**CLI depth policy:** `--bit-depth` accepts `16`, `24`, `32`, `32f`, `64f`, or `source`. With no flag, the CLI retains `source`. For DSD and lossy inputs, where a PCM source width is undefined, `source` resolves to the target format's conservative PCM default (24-bit for FLAC, ALAC, WavPack, WAV, and AIFF) and the conversion log identifies that value as a plan default. A PCM source whose original width cannot be measured still fails closed and asks for an explicit depth. Explicit numeric requests never carry the default-policy label. Twenty-bit DVD-Audio PCM is carried in a 24-bit output container, with the widening identified in the conversion log rather than misreported as a 24-bit source measurement.

-fn resolve_config_save_target(config_path: &Path) -> anyhow::Result<PathBuf> {
-    match fs::symlink_metadata(config_path) {
-        Ok(metadata) if metadata.file_type().is_symlink() => {
-            let link = fs::read_link(config_path)?;
-            Ok(if link.is_absolute() {
-                link
-            } else {
-                config_path
-                    .parent()
-                    .unwrap_or_else(|| Path::new("."))
-                    .join(link)
-            })
+pub(crate) fn resolve_config_save_target(config_path: &Path) -> anyhow::Result<PathBuf> {
+    const MAX_CONFIG_SYMLINK_DEPTH: usize = 40;
+
+    let mut current = config_path.to_path_buf();
+    let mut visited = std::collections::HashSet::new();
+
+    for depth in 0..=MAX_CONFIG_SYMLINK_DEPTH {
+        if !visited.insert(symlink_cycle_key(&current)) {
+            return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(
+                "configuration path symlink cycle detected at '{}'",
+                current.display()
+            ));
+        }
+
+        match fs::symlink_metadata(&current) {
+            Ok(metadata) if metadata.file_type().is_symlink() => {
+                if depth == MAX_CONFIG_SYMLINK_DEPTH {
+                    return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(
+                        "configuration path '{}' exceeds the maximum symlink depth of {}",
+                        config_path.display(),
+                        MAX_CONFIG_SYMLINK_DEPTH
+                    ));
+                }
+                let link = fs::read_link(&current).map_err(|error| {
+                    anyhow::anyhow!(
+                        "read configuration symlink '{}': {error}",
+                        current.display()
+                    )
+                })?;
+                current = if link.is_absolute() {
+                    link
+                } else {
+                    current
+                        .parent()
+                        .unwrap_or_else(|| Path::new("."))
+                        .join(link)
+                };
+            }
+            Ok(_) => return Ok(current),
+            Err(error) if error.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => return Ok(current),
+            Err(error) => {
+                return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(
+                    "inspect configuration path '{}': {error}",
+                    current.display()
+                ))
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    Err(anyhow::anyhow!(
+        "configuration path '{}' could not be resolved within the bounded symlink traversal",
+        config_path.display()
+    ))
+}
+
+fn symlink_cycle_key(path: &Path) -> PathBuf {
+    use std::path::Component;
+
+    let mut key = PathBuf::new();
+    for component in path.components() {
+        match component {
+            Component::CurDir => {}
+            Component::Prefix(prefix) => key.push(prefix.as_os_str()),
+            Component::RootDir => key.push(component.as_os_str()),
+            Component::ParentDir => key.push(".."),
+            Component::Normal(part) => key.push(part),
         }
-        Ok(_) | Err(_) => Ok(config_path.to_path_buf()),
+    }
+    if key.as_os_str().is_empty() {
+        PathBuf::from(".")
+    } else {
+        key
     }
 }

     parent.join(format!(".{file_name}.{suffix}"))

         let name = entry.file_name();
         let name = name.to_string_lossy();
+        let path = entry.path();
         if name.starts_with(&temp_prefix) {
-            let _ = fs::remove_file(&path);
+            stale_temps.push(path);
         } else if name.starts_with(&backup_prefix) {
-            backups.push(path);
+            backup_paths.push(path);

     }
 
-    if !target_path.exists() {

-        });
-        if let Some(backup) = backups.pop() {

+            &path,
+            "stale configuration replacement backup",

+            Ok(bytes) => backups.push(ValidatedRecoveryBackup { path, bytes }),
+            Err(error) => {
+                if first_validation_error.is_none() {
+                    first_validation_error = Some(error);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    if let Some(error) = first_validation_error {
+        return Err(error);

+        // No recovery artifacts exist, so there is nothing to arbitrate.
+        // The target's shape is the saver's concern: an atomic replace onto
+        // a non-regular target fails with the honest OS error and cleans up
+        // its temp file. Validating the target here would turn that save
+        // into a recovery error and leave the rename-failure cleanup path
+        // unreachable.

+                    "configuration recovery target '{}' is not a regular file",

+                target_path.display()

+            target_path.display()

+    }

+            )

+fn validate_recovery_artifact_is_regular(path: &Path, kind: &str) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
+    let metadata = fs::symlink_metadata(path).map_err(|error| {
+        anyhow::anyhow!("inspect {kind} '{}': {error}", path.display())
+    })?;
+    if metadata.file_type().is_symlink() || !metadata.is_file() {
+        return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(
+            "{kind} '{}' is not a regular file",
+            path.display()
+        ));
     }
     Ok(())
 }
 
+fn read_and_validate_recovery_config(path: &Path, kind: &str) -> anyhow::Result<Vec<u8>> {
+    validate_recovery_artifact_is_regular(path, kind)?;
+
+    let mut options = OpenOptions::new();
+    options.read(true);
+    #[cfg(unix)]
+    options.custom_flags(libc::O_NOFOLLOW);
+    #[cfg(windows)]
+    {
+        use std::os::windows::fs::OpenOptionsExt;
+        const FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT: u32 = 0x0020_0000;
+        options.custom_flags(FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT);
+    }
+    let mut file = options.open(path).map_err(|error| {
+        anyhow::anyhow!("open {kind} '{}' without following links: {error}", path.display())
+    })?;
+    let metadata = file.metadata().map_err(|error| {
+        anyhow::anyhow!("inspect opened {kind} '{}': {error}", path.display())
+    })?;
+    if !metadata.is_file() {
+        return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(
+            "{kind} '{}' is not a regular file",
+            path.display()
+        ));
+    }
+    let held = SameFileHandle::from_file(file.try_clone()?).map_err(|error| {
+        anyhow::anyhow!("inspect {kind} identity '{}': {error}", path.display())
+    })?;
+    let current = SameFileHandle::from_path(path).map_err(|error| {
+        anyhow::anyhow!("reopen {kind} identity '{}': {error}", path.display())
+    })?;
+    if held != current {
+        return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(
+            "{kind} '{}' changed identity during validation",
+            path.display()
+        ));
+    }
+    validate_recovery_artifact_is_regular(path, kind)?;
+
+    let mut bytes = Vec::new();
+    file.read_to_end(&mut bytes).map_err(|error| {
+        anyhow::anyhow!("read {kind} '{}': {error}", path.display())
+    })?;
+    let text = std::str::from_utf8(&bytes).map_err(|_| {
+        anyhow::anyhow!(
+            "{kind} '{}' is not a valid UTF-8 Tonepoet configuration",
+            path.display()
+        )
+    })?;
+    toml::from_str::<TonepoetConfig>(text).map_err(|_| {
+        anyhow::anyhow!(
+            "{kind} '{}' is not a valid Tonepoet configuration",
+            path.display()
+        )
+    })?;
+    Ok(bytes)
+}
+
+fn restore_config_backup_durably(
+    parent: &Path,
+    target_path: &Path,
+    backup: &ValidatedRecoveryBackup,
+) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
+    let file_name = target_path
+        .file_name()
+        .and_then(|name| name.to_str())
+        .unwrap_or("config.toml");
+    let stamp = std::time::SystemTime::now()
+        .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
+        .unwrap_or_default()
+        .as_nanos();
+    #[cfg(unix)]
+    let mode = 0o600;
+
+    let mut last_create_error = None;
+    for attempt in 0..128u32 {
+        let temporary = parent.join(format!(
+            ".{file_name}.tmp.recovery.{}.{}.{}",
+            std::process::id(),
+            stamp,
+            attempt
+        ));
+        #[cfg(unix)]
+        let opened = {
+            let mut options = OpenOptions::new();
+            options
+                .write(true)
+                .create_new(true)
+                .mode(mode & 0o777)
+                .open(&temporary)
+        };
+        #[cfg(not(unix))]
+        let opened = OpenOptions::new()
+            .write(true)
+            .create_new(true)
+            .open(&temporary);
+        let mut file = match opened {
+            Ok(file) => file,
+            Err(error) if error.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::AlreadyExists => {
+                last_create_error = Some(error);
+                continue;
+            }
+            Err(error) => {
+                return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(
+                    "create configuration recovery temporary file '{}': {error}",
+                    temporary.display()
+                ))
+            }
+        };
+
+        let publish_result = (|| -> anyhow::Result<()> {
+            file.write_all(&backup.bytes).map_err(|error| {
+                anyhow::anyhow!(
+                    "write configuration recovery temporary file '{}': {error}",
+                    temporary.display()
+                )
+            })?;
+            file.sync_all().map_err(|error| {
+                anyhow::anyhow!(
+                    "sync configuration recovery temporary file '{}': {error}",
+                    temporary.display()
+                )
+            })?;
+            drop(file);
+            fs::rename(&temporary, target_path).map_err(|error| {
+                anyhow::anyhow!(
+                    "restore configuration '{}' from replacement backup '{}': {error}",
+                    target_path.display(),
+                    backup.path.display()
+                )
+            })?;
+            sync_publication_parent_dir(parent).map_err(|error| {
+                anyhow::anyhow!(
+                    "configuration recovery replaced '{}', but parent-directory durability could not be confirmed: {error}; replacement backup '{}' was retained and secret reconciliation was not attempted",
+                    target_path.display(),
+                    backup.path.display()
+                )
+            })?;
+            Ok(())
+        })();
+
+        match publish_result {
+            Ok(()) => return Ok(()),
+            Err(error) => {
+                match fs::symlink_metadata(&temporary) {
+                    Ok(_) => {
+                        if let Err(cleanup_error) = remove_stale_config_artifact(&temporary) {
+                            return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(
+                                "{error}; additionally failed to remove recovery temporary file '{}': {cleanup_error}",
+                                temporary.display()
+                            ));
+                        }
+                    }
+                    Err(metadata_error)
+                        if metadata_error.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => {}
+                    Err(metadata_error) => {
+                        return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(
+                            "{error}; additionally failed to inspect recovery temporary file '{}' for cleanup: {metadata_error}",
+                            temporary.display()
+                        ))
+                    }
+                }
+                return Err(error);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    Err(anyhow::Error::new(last_create_error.unwrap_or_else(|| {
+        std::io::Error::new(
+            std::io::ErrorKind::AlreadyExists,
+            "could not allocate a unique configuration recovery temporary file",
+        )
+    })))
+}
+
+fn remove_stale_config_artifact(path: &Path) -> std::io::Result<()> {
+    #[cfg(test)]
+    {
+        let injected = TEST_STALE_ARTIFACT_REMOVE_FAILURE.with(|slot| {
+            let mut slot = slot.borrow_mut();
+            match slot.take() {
+                Some((target, message)) if target == path => Some(message),
+                Some(value) => {
+                    *slot = Some(value);
+                    None
+                }
+                None => None,
+            }
+        });
+        if let Some(message) = injected {
+            return Err(std::io::Error::new(std::io::ErrorKind::Other, message));
+        }
+    }
+    fs::remove_file(path)
+}

+fn create_restricted_migration_backup(source: &Path, backup: &Path) -> std::io::Result<()> {
+    let source_bytes = std::fs::read(source)?;
+    let created = if backup.exists() {

+            return Err(std::io::Error::new(
+                std::io::ErrorKind::AlreadyExists,
+                format!(
+                    "existing migration backup '{}' does not match current source '{}'",
+                    backup.display(),
+                    source.display()
+                ),
+            ));
+        }
+        false
     } else {
-        Ok(())
+        match crate::secret_store::atomic_write_private_file(backup, &source_bytes)? {
+            crate::secret_store::PrivateFilePublishOutcome::Durable => true,
+            crate::secret_store::PrivateFilePublishOutcome::ReplacedButDurabilityUnconfirmed(
+                detail,
+            ) => {
+                return Err(std::io::Error::new(
+                    std::io::ErrorKind::Other,
+                    format!(
+                        "cleartext migration backup was replaced but is not durably published: {detail}"
+                    ),
+                ))
+            }
+        }
+    };
+    #[cfg(unix)]
+    {
+        if let Err(error) = std::fs::set_permissions(
+            backup,
+            std::fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o600),
+        ) {
+            let cleanup_error = created
+                .then(|| std::fs::remove_file(backup).err())
+                .flatten();
+            return Err(migration_backup_permission_error(
+                backup,
+                error,
+                cleanup_error,
+            ));
+        }

+    Ok(())

+fn published_config_secret_references(config_path: &Path) -> anyhow::Result<Vec<String>> {

+    /// Load config from an explicit path. Cleartext legacy passwords are
+    /// migrated while holding the same cross-process lock across authoritative
+    /// reads, journal reconciliation, keyring mutation, config publication, and
+    /// journal retirement.
+    pub fn load_from_path<P: AsRef<Path>>(config_path: P) -> anyhow::Result<Self> {
+        let (_lock, target_path) = StoreFileLock::acquire_for_path(config_path.as_ref())?;
+        recover_config_artifacts_locked(&target_path)?;
+        Self::load_from_locked_path(&target_path)
+    }
+
+    fn load_from_locked_path(config_path: &Path) -> anyhow::Result<Self> {
+        let _published_references = reconcile_config_secret_publication_locked(config_path)?;
+        if !config_path.exists() {
+            return Ok(Self::default());
+        }
+
+        let content = std::fs::read_to_string(config_path)?;
+        let mut config: TonepoetConfig = toml::from_str(&content)?;
+
+        if let Some(cleartext) = config.conversion.archive_password.clone() {
+            let backup = config_path.with_extension("toml.pre-keychain-migration");
+            let mut pending_reference = None;
+            let reference = if let Some(reference) = config.conversion.archive_password_ref.as_deref() {
+                let stored = crate::secret_store::get(reference).map_err(anyhow::Error::new)?;
+                if stored != cleartext {
+                    return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(
+                        "archive-password migration is ambiguous: config cleartext and secret reference disagree"
+                    ));
+                }
+                reference.to_string()
+            } else {
+                let reference = next_config_secret_reference(config_path, None)?;
+                crate::secret_store::begin_pending_publication(
+                    config_path,
+                    std::slice::from_ref(&reference),
+                )
+                .map_err(anyhow::Error::msg)?;
+                if let Err(store_error) = crate::secret_store::set(&reference, &cleartext) {
+                    let primary = anyhow::anyhow!(store_error);
+                    return match crate::secret_store::abort_pending_publication(
+                        config_path,
+                        std::slice::from_ref(&reference),
+                    ) {
+                        Ok(()) => Err(primary),
+                        Err(cleanup_error) => Err(anyhow::anyhow!(
+                            "{primary}; additionally {cleanup_error}"
+                        )),
+                    };
+                }
+                pending_reference = Some(reference.clone());
+                reference
+            };
+
+            if let Err(backup_error) = create_restricted_migration_backup(config_path, &backup) {
+                let Some(reference) = pending_reference else {
+                    return Err(backup_error.into());
+                };
+                return match crate::secret_store::abort_pending_publication(
+                    config_path,
+                    std::slice::from_ref(&reference),
+                ) {
+                    Ok(()) => Err(backup_error.into()),
+                    Err(cleanup_error) => Err(anyhow::anyhow!(
+                        "could not create restricted archive-password migration backup '{}': {backup_error}; additionally {cleanup_error}",
+                        backup.display()
+                    )),
+                };
+            }
+
+            config.conversion.archive_password_ref = Some(reference.clone());
+            let mut persisted = config.clone();
+            persisted.conversion.archive_password = None;
+            let save_outcome = match toml::to_string_pretty(&persisted)
+                .map_err(anyhow::Error::new)
+                .and_then(|content| atomic_write_config_locked(config_path, content.as_bytes()))
+            {
+                Ok(outcome) => outcome,
+                Err(save_error) => {
+                    let Some(reference) = pending_reference else {
+                        return Err(save_error);
+                    };
+                    return match crate::secret_store::abort_pending_publication(
+                        config_path,
+                        std::slice::from_ref(&reference),
+                    ) {
+                        Ok(()) => Err(save_error),
+                        Err(cleanup_error) => Err(anyhow::anyhow!(
+                            "{save_error}; additionally {cleanup_error}"
+                        )),
+                    };
+                }
+            };
+            match save_outcome {
+                ConfigSaveOutcome::Durable => {
+                    if pending_reference.is_some() {
+                        crate::secret_store::reconcile_pending_publication(
+                            config_path,
+                            std::slice::from_ref(&reference),
+                        )
+                        .map_err(anyhow::Error::msg)?;
+                    }
+                }
+                ConfigSaveOutcome::ReplacedButDurabilityUnconfirmed(detail) => {
+                    let authority = if pending_reference.is_some() {
+                        "the pending secret-publication journal was retained for reconciliation"
+                    } else {
+                        "no new secret reference was created"
+                    };
+                    return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(
+                        "archive-password migration was replaced but is not durably published: {detail}; {authority}"
+                    ));
+                }
+            }
+        } else if let Some(reference) = config.conversion.archive_password_ref.as_deref() {
+            config.conversion.archive_password = Some(
+                crate::secret_store::get(reference).map_err(anyhow::Error::new)?,
+            );
+        }
+        Ok(config)
+    }
+
+    /// Explicitly request removal of the configured archive-password authority
+    /// on the next save. A reference-only state retains the existing authority;
+    /// clearing both fields requests durable reference removal and retirement.
+    pub fn clear_archive_password(&mut self) {
+        self.conversion.archive_password = None;
+        self.conversion.archive_password_ref = None;
+    }
+

+    ///
+    /// This convenience API requires confirmed durable publication. If the
+    /// destination was replaced but its parent-directory update could not be
+    /// confirmed durable, the method returns that structured detail as an
+    /// error; callers must not report ordinary save success.
     ///
-    /// The write is serialized with an OS-backed save lock, uses a
-    /// same-directory temporary file, fsyncs it, atomically publishes it on
-    /// supported platforms, preserves an existing file's permissions, follows a
-    /// final symlink to support dotfile-managed configs, and fsyncs the parent
-    /// directory when the platform exposes that durability primitive.
+    /// The entire secret-publication transaction is serialized by one OS lock:
+    /// authoritative read, journal reconciliation, keyring mutation, atomic file
+    /// publication, and journal retirement all occur under the same ownership.
     pub fn save(&self) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
-        self.save_with_outcome().map(|_| ())
+        require_durable_config_save(self.save_with_outcome()?)
     }
 
     pub fn save_with_outcome(&self) -> anyhow::Result<ConfigSaveOutcome> {
@@ -776,26 +1651,196 @@ impl TonepoetConfig {
 
     /// Save config to an explicit path. This exists so UI persistence paths can
     /// be tested against temporary config files without mutating the user's

+    /// real configuration. Like `save`, it returns an error after replacement
+    /// when publication durability cannot be confirmed.
     pub fn save_to_path<P: AsRef<Path>>(&self, config_path: P) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
